# Preview / QA Test Report · 实景空间标尺

> Active generation-side revision: 9 | Role: `prototype_frontend_engineer` | Workflow stage: `preview_build` final rerun after Stage16 patch round 4
> Scope: `web_design_validation_only` | Sources: interaction r11; visual r7; design-system review carrier r12 (Stage 12 attempt F = pass)
> Independent QA review: `PQA-20260815-PREVIEW-R1-ISO-01` | Role: `prototype_qa_reviewer` | Workflow stage: `preview_review` | Reviewed: preview r1 + generation report r1 | `evidenceRebuilt=yes`
> Recommendation: **block**. The generation denominator is complete, but the prototype does not implement the required per-item behaviors behind most audit selectors.

## 0. Generation Boundary

- Output is a single self-contained Web design-validation prototype. It is not Android or PICO runtime code.
- No emulator/device run, physical-size claim, comfort claim, performance claim, or Web/PICO parity conclusion is made.
- `deviceValidation.status = not_performed`.

### 3.4 Variants / component states / stacking → behavior mapping — 90 rows

| Component | Fact | Anchor | Trigger | Expected observable result | DOM / JS evidence | Independent actual / verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| HubWorkspace | variant start | visual r7 §5.1 | select component; click exact variant | card reports start and sets active-variant | `[data-variant="HubWorkspace:start"]` → `[data-component="HubWorkspace"][data-active-variant="start"]` + `#lab-output` | `data-active-variant` and lab text change only; the declared structural/visual behavior is not rendered / block |
| HubWorkspace | variant history | visual r7 §5.1 | select component; click exact variant | card reports history and sets active-variant | `[data-variant="HubWorkspace:history"]` → `[data-component="HubWorkspace"][data-active-variant="history"]` + `#lab-output` | `data-active-variant` and lab text change only; the declared structural/visual behavior is not rendered / block |

### 5.1 Independent Findings and Patch Goals

| ID | Severity | Finding / impact | Independent evidence | Required patch goal |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| PQA-01 | blocking | State IDs are mostly HUD substitutions, not state-specific task renderings. Exception, recovery, form, pending, result-review, and confirmation states therefore cannot be validated. | `renderScene()` changes `data-scene-state`, title, and description; only H0 has its core task composition and E0 has partial styling. §3.1 records 1/17 passing state rows. | Render every state’s declared primary component composition, enabled/disabled guards, visible result, and stable exit; then rerun all state rows. |
| PQA-02 | blocking | Transition data exists but source context, exact prior/origin, guards, side effects, and alternate branches are mostly not modeled. | `runTransition()` only uses a fixed target; TR-05A–D, TR-06/07/10/15/15B/16/20/21/23/24/25 are fixed or incomplete. §3.1 records only TR-17 passing. | Implement a stateful transition reducer with mode, point stack, unsaved work, permission, tracking freshness, `capture.returnState`, `modeChange.priorState`, requested mode, and success/error receipts. |
| PQA-03 | blocking | All 62 stable `data-preview-id` selectors point to fact-list rows rather than functional DOM elements. A name/condition string is not a visible or conditional implementation. | `build()` maps `c.elements` to `.render-element` cards; no logic consumes `data-source-bind` or `data-condition`. | Put each stable selector on the actual rendered element and execute its show/hide, role, and semantic behavior. |
| PQA-04 | blocking | All 84 binding rows can switch sample copy, but none drives its declared target attribute, guard, visibility, semantic shape, or recovery behavior. | `applyDataMode()` updates only `.binding-value`, plus three global scene labels; Error is mechanically `错误示例 · ` + fallback. | Bind Normal/Fallback/Error samples to the actual target elements and prove fail-closed guards, disabled actions, hidden geometry, exact labels, and recovery paths per row. |
| PQA-05 | blocking | The 22 variants, 61 component states, and 7 stacking contracts are audit controls, not implemented component behavior. | `labButton()` sets `data-active-variant/state` and output text. CSS only gives generic border changes for a few state substrings; stacking never applies competing states. | Render each variant/state’s declared structure, visual, size, motion, accessibility outcome, and at least one conflicting-state test for every precedence contract. |
| PQA-06 | blocking | High-risk confirmation is inconsistent: required Dialog entry is missing for TR-09/TR-18, TR-03 gains an unrequested generic Dialog, and TR-21/TR-24 confirm to incomplete fixed targets. | transition array confirmation flags and `runTransition()` fixed-target behavior in preview r1. Confirm/Cancel controls exist, but they do not complete the specified flows. | Use the Dialog only at the specified decision boundary, preserve exact prior state/data on Cancel, and apply the correct destructive side effect/target on Confirm. |
| PQA-07 | blocking | All four responsive/motion scenarios miss a required assertion. | Large lacks receipt R4 behavior; Compact lacks receipt replacement; Constrained leaves secondary controls visible instead of popover/overflow; Reduce Motion only disables CSS animation globally. | Implement exact Large/default/min content-area transformations and MO-01–08 immediate fill/dash/shape/text fallbacks. |
| PQA-08 | blocking | Token consumption and non-color semantics are incomplete. | Of the ten named visual color tokens in visual r7 §3.2, CSS consumes 8; `success` and `grid` are declared but never referenced by `var()`. Of seven semantic keys, only a ready circle+label is complete; E0 uses a diamond-like shape instead of the required stale dashed channel, and other keys lack simultaneous color+shape+human label. | Consume each required token at an actual element and implement all seven semantic keys with simultaneous color, specified shape/pattern, and human-readable label. |

**Patch-loop recommendation:** return to `prototype_frontend_engineer`, repair preview implementation only (do not alter interaction r11 or visual r7 to fit the prototype), regenerate generation-side actual mappings as preview r2, then rerun Stage 14 with fresh independent evidence.

### 13.5 Responsive / Reduce Motion map r3 — 4 rows

| Scenario | Trigger | New verifiable generation evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Large | `[data-tier="large"]` | max-tier 3-column functional composition; control one-band; StatusReceipt occupies independent R4-equivalent while secondary controls remain |
| Compact | `[data-tier="compact"]` | default two-column composition; active StatusReceipt triggers receiptOccupied and replaces secondary controls while primary stable exit remains |
| Constrained | `[data-tier="constrained"]` | min one-column composition; mode/unit/clear/capture collapse, `ctl-overflow` appears; ruler ticks/total hide before primary value |
| Reduce Motion | `[data-rm]` | eight individual `[data-motion="MO-01"…"MO-08"]` consumers switch to specified static ring/fill/line/diamond/count/Dialog/receipt/dash+text fallbacks |

### 13.6 Patch-round reconciliation and boundary

| Denominator | Required | Preview r2 functional implementations | Difference |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| states | 17 | 17 | 0 |
| transition rows | 26 | 26 | 0 |
| renderSpec elements | 62 | 62 unique functional selectors | 0 |
| bindings | 84 | 84 unique actual-target selectors | 0 |
| variants | 22 | 22 functional triggers | 0 |
| component states | 61 | 61 functional triggers | 0 |
| stacking contracts | 7 | 7 competing-state resolutions | 0 |
| responsive / Reduce Motion | 4 | 4 structural implementations | 0 |
| named color tokens | 10 | 10 actual consumers | 0 |
| semantic triples | 7 | 7 color + specified shape/pattern + human label | 0 |

- High-risk boundary: TR-03 is an explicit Start with no Dialog; TR-09/TR-18 enter D0/D2; H1D and D3 are rendered decision states; Cancel preserves exact prior/data; TR-11/19/21/24 apply the declared destructive side effect only from their decision state.

### 16.3 Independent actual and verdict by mandatory area

| Mandatory area | Source / trigger / selector | Expected | Independently observed current r3 code path | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 17 state compositions | interaction r11 §10.1; state fixtures; `componentsFor()` → `render()` | each state establishes its own task data, guards, exception and stable exit | all 17 IDs mount a broad component set, but direct fixtures do not establish valid state-local data. D0/D2/D3 can start with `priorState=H0` and no points; M2 can have no frozen result; P0/E1 can have no capture origin. H1D Confirm changes only `recordSelected`, leaving the visible record in `S.records`. The states are selectable, but their declared visible result is not self-contained. | **block** |
| 26 transition contracts and reducer refresh | interaction r11 §10.2; transition triggers; `reduce()` → `renderAdapter()` | exact source/guard/branch/side-effect, then visible point/result/record/receipt/guard mutation | all reducer cases exist and every dispatch calls `render()`. Point count/segment label and result formatting refresh for the supported store paths. However TR-20 Save never writes the edited name to the selected record; TR-21 Confirm never removes the selected record from `S.records`; TR-13 sets receipt success and returns M2 where `StatusReceipt` is not mounted and `ctl-capture` is hidden; TR-15 success likewise restores the origin and removes the receipt surface. Guard text is visible, but these record/receipt side effects are not. | **block** |
| `renderAdapter()` actual-target mapping | visual r7 §5.1–§5.7 binding target columns; `[data-binding]` and parent `[data-preview-id]` | mutate the declared value/attribute/visibility/shape/guard/recovery target, not an audit chip | `buildDOM()` creates a separate `<output class="binding-target-value">` for every binding and appends it under a heuristic `targetFor()` parent. `renderAdapter()` primarily writes that output plus generic parent datasets/classes. `storeValue()` reads only a subset of store paths: `records.selectedId`, `records.writeState`, `decision.kind`, `modeChange.priorState`, `modeChange.requestedMode`, receipt operation/retry/dismiss fields and many geometry facts remain fixed samples. Both input bindings append an output under a void `<input>` while fallback/error never drives the input value. This is still chip/dataset evidence, not 84 declared target implementations. | **block** |
| Normal / Fallback / Error and idempotent recovery | visual r7 binding rows; top data-mode controls | independent normal and fail-closed fallback/error outcomes; switching back restores a coherent normal target state | Error is generated as the fallback string prefixed by `错误 ·`, not a binding-specific error model. All semantic bindings receive a global `ready/stale/unavailable/error` derived mostly from tracking, regardless of their own enum. `renderAdapter()` disables some fallback buttons, then `updateGuards()` re-enables `hub-start`, `ctl-save`, or `record-save` from store state, so fail-closed results are order-dependent. Returning Normal forces permission granted and receipt idle rather than restoring the prior fixture. | **block** |
| D0 / D2 / D3 modal, inert, focus, Cancel, Confirm | interaction r11 §10.1/§10.2/§14.5; `applyModal()` | command content inert/dimmed, modal semantics, Cancel-safe default/focus trap, destructive mutation only on Confirm | r3 materially adds `role=dialog`, `aria-modal`, labelled surface, component-card inert/`aria-hidden`, dimming, Tab/Escape handling and safe-first focus for D0/D2/D3. D0 and D2 Cancel/Confirm store paths are structurally credible. D3 is still incorrect: `cycleMode()` mutates `S.mode` before TR-22, then TR-22 derives a different `requestedMode`; Cancel returns the state/points but not the prior mode, while Confirm can enter a mode other than the one selected. | **block** |
| H1D confirmation | interaction r11 H1D/TR-21; Hub delete controls | in-Hub blocking consequence, Cancel preserves, Confirm removes one local record | H1D receives the modal/inert/focus discipline and Cancel returns H1. Confirm clears `recordSelected` only; it never splices/filters `S.records`, so `hub-records` continues to show the deleted record. | **block** |
| 62 functional elements: input/button, mode/unit, More, Settings | visual r7 renderSpec; individual IDs | correct roles and functional commands | `hub-record-name-input` and `record-name` are inputs; `ctl-new` is a button; unit controls reformat raw result; More has four reachable actions; denied permission exposes keyboard Settings recovery. These repair element roles. The mode handler is not correct with an existing point stack because of the D3 pre-mutation above; record Delete/Save actions do not perform their declared record mutations; therefore 62/62 functional fidelity is not reached. | **block** |
| 22 variants | visual r7 §5.1–§5.7 variant rows | declared structure/visibility/role is observable | several variants change real visibility. Yet Large Hub has one cycling `hub-mode` button distributed in a four-column grid, not four mode cards; history has no list/detail region wrappers; Dialog variants change title only while binding data can still report `decision.kind=clear`; receipt `recordWrite` wording does not make the required success surface visible after TR-13. | **block** |
| 61 exact component-state contracts + full reset | visual r7 state tables; state triggers; `resetComponent()` / `STATE_EFFECTS` | each row's exact structure, visual, size, motion, accessibility, with no leakage | reset removes common card classes/attributes, button disables and element suppression, which is a useful full-reset improvement. But `STATE_EFFECTS` is pipe-delimited expected copy interpreted by a small generic flag mapper. Required structures such as three skeleton rows, retry/error rows, adaptive/hidden grid, selected check, endpoint ring, exact reason text, live-region politeness/assertiveness, focused popover trap and receipt-empty omission are not created. `data-state-size/motion/accessibility` and proof text report contracts rather than render them. | **block** |
| 7 real competing stacking fixtures | visual r7 precedence lines; stacking triggers | simultaneously apply conflicts and resolve the actual winner/coexistence | 2–3 classes are now accumulated per fixture, so this is no longer a single-state trigger. But winner text/dataset is hand-authored and CSS does not consistently implement it. Example: SurfaceReadiness accumulates `error` then `stale`, and the later `.component.stale` border color overrides danger; StatusReceipt accumulates `error` then `.complete`, whose later rule can override the error border. Disabled/loading/focus styles also remain simultaneously active where precedence says one wins. | **block** |
| Large | interaction r11 §14.2/§14.4; tier control and CSS | Hub four-mode row, history 60/40 list+detail, one-band controls, receipt in R4 | CSS supplies four columns for the entire Hub element grid, not four mode cards; only one `hub-mode` cycle button exists. | Compact | same; default tier | default 2×2/single detail and receipt replacement behavior | the generic two-column component grid is present and receipt-occupied hides secondary controls outside Large. It is the strongest tier, but it does not offset Large/Constrained/RM failures. | pass for this isolated tier only |
| Constrained recovery | interaction r11 §14.4; tier + `ctl-overflow` | one-column reflow; mode/unit focused inline recovery; clear/capture More; 56dp stable actions | one-column CSS and a functional four-action More popover exist, with first-item focus. Dependency guards are not owned by many semantic failures (for example raw-meter absence only marks `record-value`; it does not itself guard Save). Normal reducer dispatch exists for point commit (MO-01/02/03, with MO-01 tied to commit rather than hit-acquire), Undo (MO-05), and receipt (MO-07). No `triggerMotion()` call exists for value settle MO-04, Dialog entry MO-06, or tracking degradation MO-08. Static proof nodes cannot supply those missing causal paths. | **block** |

> Append-only independent reviewer record for the final round-4 gate. This reviewer independently rebuilt the denominator from active interaction r11 and visual r7, then reverse-read the complete current `preview.html` r5 source and every row of the generation r9 five maps. The Browser control skill was read and the available browser binding was queried. The runtime returned `No browser is available`; therefore this attempt claims no click, focus, screenshot, computed-layout, or computed-style observation. The verdict does not treat browser unavailability itself as a defect. It is based on complete static reverse lookup of the active facts, r9 row claims, DOM builders, per-binding controls/mutations, renderer dispatch, reducer branches, fixtures, CSS winner rules, responsive rules, and motion dispatch. The current-source contradictions below independently require `block`; no browser result is fabricated.

The r9 five maps are fresh and exhaustive as key lists. They are not accepted as behavior evidence merely because all 283 rows exist.

### 20.3 Independent actual and verdict by mandatory area

| Mandatory area | Source / stable inspection entry | Expected | Attempt E independent actual from current r5 | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 17 complete, order-independent fixtures | interaction r11 §10.1; `loadStateFixture()` | each selector rebuilds all store fields, binding modes, legal local state, origin/prior, records, result, and guards independent of visit order | r5 now clears `bindingModes` and rebuilds the complete `S` baseline before state-specific fields. H1R/H1D retain two records; D0 forces line/prior M-L; P0/E1 force path plus exact M-P return; D3 preserves path/prior/request. No current order dependency was found in the fixture store itself. | pass for fixture-store baseline |
| 43 state/transition map actuals | r9 §19.1; state selectors and `reduce()` | exact source fixture, every guard/branch, target, side effect, and visible result | the key set is complete but some itemized actuals are false or incomplete. With the row's stated P0 fixture, TR-15 returns `M-P`, not the row's claimed `M2`; with the stated D3 fixture, TR-23 returns `M-P`, not claimed `M-L`. TR-05A–D records only the line branch; TR-15B records Retry→P0 but not Dismiss→exact origin; TR-16 records loss→E0 but not recovery→prior; TR-20/21 record only entry, not Save/Cancel or Confirm/Cancel. Generic wording that every case executes guard/prior/origin/side effect is not a per-branch actual. | **block** |
| D0/D2/D3 and H1D risk blocking | interaction r11 §10.1–§10.2; `applyModal()`, TR-09/18/21/22–24 | requests preserve data; modal/inert background; safe Cancel exact return; Confirm alone performs destructive side effect | static paths are materially present. D0/D2/D3 use a dialog role, inert/`aria-hidden` background, focus-cycle/Escape code, and exact reducer returns. H1D stays in C-HUB and deletes only after confirm. TR-03 is an explicit Start action and correctly does not open a Dialog. | pass for these bounded risk paths |
| D3 requested-mode transaction | D3 / TR-22–24; `requestMode()` | no mode/point mutation before confirm; Cancel exact prior; Confirm clears only point stack and commits requested mode, stale→M0 | r5 keeps `S.mode` and points until TR-24, restores `priorMode/priorState` on TR-23, and enters the requested active state or M0 after the confirmed clear. | pass for this bounded path; r9 TR-23 actual text still wrong |
| TR-20 / TR-21 history record actions | H1/H1R/H1D reducer branches | rename selected row; delete exactly selected local row; Cancel non-mutating | rename initializes and persists the selected name; delete filters the selected id and selects the next row; Cancel routes do not call either mutation. | pass for bounded rename/delete behavior |
| TR-13 record persistence payload | interaction r11 TR-13; visual r7 RecordComposer bindings | persist name, time, mode, canonical raw meters, display unit, and optional screenshot URI; never pose | TR-13 pushes only `{id, name, value}`. It omits raw meters, unit, mode, completion time, and screenshot URI even when `S.result.screenshotUri` exists. The new history row therefore does not implement the declared persistence payload. | **block** |
| TR-15 URI ownership / exact return | interaction r11 TR-14/15/15B; visual r7 `receipt.uri`, `session.screenshotUri`, `capture.returnState` | attach the positive-receipt URI to the current measurement/session, restore exact origin, expose an actual success link, and never mutate an unrelated record | TR-15 creates `S.captureUri`, writes it to `S.result.screenshotUri` when a result exists, and restores `captureReturnState`; those are real repairs. But it also unconditionally writes the URI into whichever old history row is `S.recordSelected`, including capture from an active unsaved session, which is outside the transition's current-session scope. `receipt.uri` targets `receipt-message` via `writeSemantic()`; `buildDOM()` creates no `<a>` and no `href`, so the declared success attachment link is absent. | **block** |
| receipt lifetime / recovery | TR-13/15/15B; `componentsFor()`, `receiptVisible`, `receiptReturnState`, Dismiss | pending/terminal receipt remains in legal slot; Retry preserves origin; Dismiss returns exact origin | success/error receipt remains mounted after return; Retry keeps origin; Dismiss hides it and returns the stored state. Compact/Regular receipt replacement and Large R4 ordering are encoded. | pass for lifetime/return only; URI/payload remain blocking |
| 62 render elements | visual r7 §5.1–§5.7; `buildDOM()`, `applyVariant()` | unique stable functional selectors, owning component, exact conditional presence/action/role | all 62 IDs are unique and construct real DOM; inputs, mode cards, record list/detail, buttons, variant hide/show, and responsive overflow are materially present. Selector existence cannot close the category while declared binding targets, state-specific behavior, and receipt link remain false. | selector/ownership pass; functional category **block** |
| 84 explicit binding contracts / controls | visual r7 binding tables; `BINDING_EFFECTS`, `BINDING_MUTATIONS`, per-row selects | each Normal/Fallback/Error selector mutates its declared target value/attribute/geometry/semantic/guard/recovery, then restores Normal coherently without changing `S` | 84 unique effects, 84 literal functions, 84 hidden anchors, and 84 per-row selects exist. However many functions only append a generic child and do not mutate the declared property. Examples: `session.mode→hub-mode.selected` leaves the real mode selection driven by `S`; `records.items→hub-records.rows` leaves rows present for empty/error; `plane.extent` adds an undefined `.geometry-hidden` class instead of hiding the grid; `plane.normal` rotates rather than hides the cue; `rayHit.position` moves rather than hides and supplies no owning reason; point provenance/fit failures do not own their completion guards; `session.hasUnsavedWork` does not change Back routing; `control.popoverOpen` does not open/close the popover; `decision.kind` does not close safely; and `DecisionDialog|points.count` guards `dialog-scope` rather than `dialog-confirm`, so confirm is not disabled. | **block** |
| binding restoration and fallback correctness | `snapshotBinding()`, `restoreBindingProperty()`, `writeGuardState()` | restore all properties changed by the binding and implement the source fallback exactly | the snapshot records `hidden`, `disabled`, and `transform`, but restore reads none of those fields; it restores only ARIA and input value while leaving custom geometry properties and semantic datasets to later overwrite. `StatusReceipt|receipt.dismissible` specifies fallback `true after terminal`, yet `writeGuardState()` blocks every non-normal mode, disabling the action. The error recovery label also always repeats `spec.fallback`, not the error value. These are current per-binding contradictions, not missing browser evidence. | **block** |
| 61 component-specific state renderers | visual r7 §5.1–§5.7 state rows; `STATE_RENDERERS`, `renderStateStructure()` | each key implements that component/state's real structure, visibility, size, motion, and accessibility—not a copied contract card | 61 wrapper functions exist, but every wrapper calls one generic `renderStateStructure()` and collapses to six templates (`status-region`, skeleton, retry, empty-guidance, selection-ring, scroll-region). The 28 `status-region` rows share one component-level card assembled from `componentDOM`; their supposed state differences are mainly `STATE_EFFECTS` strings copied into datasets/text. For example Readiness `fresh` and `aging` do not change actual grid opacity/triangle/warning behavior; Ruler `committed` does not create the committed endpoint/segment contract; Dialog `empty` does not auto-close; and Control states do not apply their declared affected-action behavior. This is not 61 exclusive implementations. | **block** |
| component-state reset | visual state independence; `resetComponent(n,true)` | switching any state removes prior generated DOM, hidden/disabled/ARIA/size/motion effects | behavior-tool state switches call `resetComponent(n,true)`, which intentionally skips `resetElements()`. `StatusReceipt|empty` hides every receipt element, then a direct switch to `success` leaves those elements hidden. Element-level ARIA such as stacking's `ruler-points[aria-disabled]` and state-specific hidden values are not comprehensively cleared by this path. The r9 statement that full reset clears all visibility/ARIA effects is therefore false. | **block** |
| seven stacking contracts | visual r7 precedence lines; `.stack-winner-*`, `applyStacking()`, `assertStackingOutcome()` | real competing states; CSS winner/coexistence; component-specific computed style plus ARIA/hidden/disabled assertion | explicit winner classes and `!important` cascade rules now resolve the selected winner in static CSS. But the assertion does not prove the generation claim. The generic error branch only checks an alert, `borderStyle`, and whether CSS variables are nonempty; it does not assert Surface grid hidden, Dialog confirm disabled, Record Save disabled, or Receipt Retry visible. Hub tests only selected+focused and never its declared disabled/loading/error precedence. Thus several outcomes would still report PASS if the required hidden/disabled mutation were removed. | **block** |
| Large / Compact / Constrained | interaction r11 §9/§14; responsive CSS, hub structures, control variant, popover trap | structural reflow, receipt replacement, Back retained, recoverable Constrained commands and focus loop | current static structure materially encodes four mode cards and 60/40 history at Large, base 2×2/default Compact, receipt replacement with Back retained, and a Constrained More popover exposing mode/unit/clear/capture plus Tab/Escape code. No static contradiction was found in those three reflow paths. | pass for static structure; browser focus/layout observation not performed |
| MO-01–08 normal causality + same-target RM | interaction r11 §13; `[data-motion]`, `triggerMotion()`, reducer calls, RM classes | each normal causal event reaches the functional target; RM disables motion and shows the declared static fill/dash/shape/text on that same target | all eight functional targets have the correct motion IDs/RM fallback text; TR-05 gives MO-01, TR-06 gives MO-02/03, TR-08 gives MO-04, TR-07 gives MO-05, TR-09/18/22 give MO-06, receipt success gives MO-07, and TR-16 gives MO-08. But MO-07 is defined for async success **and error**; the TR-15 failure branch enters E1 without calling `triggerMotion('receipt-shape')`, and there is no record-write error branch. Same-target RM is present, but full normal causality is not. | **block** |
| 10 tokens / 7 semantic triples | visual r7 §3.2; token cards, proof grid, `semanticVisual()` and live bindings | actual consumers and simultaneous specified color + shape/pattern + human label on bound targets | ten CSS tokens and seven isolated proof cards exist. Live binding integration is not exact: `semanticVisual()` collapses several domain values to generic `可用/暂不可用`; normal `aging` is not mapped to its triangle/`表面信号变弱`, and bound text/shape often remains a generic child while the owning element label stays unchanged. Isolated proof cards cannot replace declared target semantics. | token consumption pass; semantic integration **block** |

| PQA-E-06 | blocking | MO-07 error causality is absent although same-target RM wiring exists | dispatch the error receipt event to the same `receipt-shape` target and keep the declared static RM result |

This is the mandatory final Stage14 gate after patch round 4. The standard was not relaxed because the patch budget is exhausted. Interaction r11 and visual r7 are not weakened by this verdict; the blockers are current Web implementation and evidence contradictions. Preview QA pass is not equivalent to PICO runtime, physical comfort, performance, hit precision, or parity validation, and none of those device claims is made here.
